# DGX Spark: gb10-overnight / general

Captured 2026-11-26T01:20:06.889342+00:00 (UTC); **stopped: deadline**. Supervisor deadline: 2026-11-26T09:00:00+08:00.

2433 of 2490 indexed cases passed all three backends. 87 records lack an archived source tree. [Portable evidence](campaign-final-general.json.gz) retains indexed failures, discovery/holdout samples and both host/device samples where recorded.

## Results by compiler

Compare only within a row. Ratios are medians of paired case ratios (Inductor / SparkFuse), not model-weighted speedups. Device = warm CUDA Graph replay; host = synchronized Python invocation. Neither is serving throughput. Admitted means a held-out gain vs the compiler’s own default, not a win over Inductor.
